function initApp() {
  var c = STATE.comercial;
  document.getElementById('comercialName').textContent = c.nombre.split(' ')[0];
  document.getElementById('welcomeName').textContent = c.nombre;
  document.getElementById('profileName').textContent = c.nombre;
  document.getElementById('profileZona').textContent = c.zona;
  document.getElementById('profileAvatar').textContent = c.nombre.charAt(0).toUpperCase();
  var now = new Date();
  document.getElementById('welcomeDate').textContent = now.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {weekday:'long', day:'numeric', month:'long'});
  updateStats();
  renderCatalogo();
  renderKitsGrid();
  calcularSim();
  initSignature();
  renderVisitas();
}

function navTo(section) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(function(s) { s.classList.remove('active'); });
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(function(n) { n.classList.remove('active'); });
  var screenMap = { inicio:'screen-inicio', visita:'screen-visita', catalogo:'screen-catalogo', simulador:'screen-simulador', visitas:'screen-visitas', perfil:'screen-perfil' };
  var screenId = screenMap[section] || ('screen-' + section);
  var screen = document.getElementById(screenId);
  if (screen) screen.classList.add('active');
  var navEl = document.getElementById('nav-' + section);
  if (navEl) navEl.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('appMain').scrollTop = 0;
  var subtitles = { inicio:'App Comercial', visita:'Nueva visita', catalogo:'Catálogo', simulador:'Simulador', visitas:'Mis visitas', perfil:'Mi perfil' };
  document.getElementById('headerSubtitle').textContent = subtitles[section] || 'App Comercial';
}

function updateStats() {
  var today = new Date().toDateString();
  var thisMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);
  var visitas = STATE.visitas;
  var hoy = 0; var contratos = 0; var mes = 0;
  visitas.forEach(function(v) {
    if (new Date(v.fecha).toDateString() === today) hoy++;
    if (v.estado === 'aprobado') contratos++;
    if (v.fecha.startsWith(thisMonth) && v.estado === 'aprobado') mes++;
  });
  var conv = visitas.length > 0 ? Math.round((contratos / visitas.length) * 100) : 0;
  document.getElementById('statVisitas').textContent = hoy;
  document.getElementById('statContratos').textContent = contratos;
  document.getElementById('statMes').textContent = mes;
  document.getElementById('profileMes').textContent = mes;
  document.getElementById('profileTotal').textContent = contratos;
  document.getElementById('profileConv').textContent = conv + '%';
  var objetivo = 5;
  document.getElementById('objetivoActual').textContent = mes + '/' + objetivo;
  var pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((mes / objetivo) * 100));
  document.getElementById('progBar').style.width = pct + '%';
  document.getElementById('progLabel').textContent = pct + '% objetivo mensual';
  renderUltimasVisitas();
}

function renderUltimasVisitas() {
  var cont = document.getElementById('ultimasVisitas');
  var today = new Date().toDateString();
  var hoy = STATE.visitas.filter(function(v) { return new Date(v.fecha).toDateString() === today; }).slice(-3).reverse();
  if (!hoy.length) {
    cont.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text2);font-size:0.82rem;padding:20px 0"><i class="fas fa-inbox" style="font-size:2rem;opacity:0.3;display:block;margin-bottom:8px"></i>Aún no hay visitas hoy</div>';
    return;
  }
  cont.innerHTML = hoy.map(function(v) {
    var badgeCls = v.estado === 'aprobado' ? 'badge-green' : (v.estado === 'rechazado' ? 'badge-red' : 'badge-orange');
    var badgeTxt = v.estado === 'aprobado' ? '✓ Aprobado' : (v.estado === 'rechazado' ? '✗ Rechaz.' : 'Pendiente');
    return '<div class="list-item"><div class="list-avatar"><i class="fas fa-user"></i></div><div class="list-info"><div class="list-name">' + escHtml(v.nombre) + '</div><div class="list-sub">' + escHtml(v.kit) + ' · ' + escHtml(v.hora) + '</div></div><span class="badge ' + badgeCls + '">' + badgeTxt + '</span></div>';
  }).join('');
}

function iniciarVisita() {
  STATE.currentStep = 1;
  STATE.kycData = {};
  STATE.selectedKit = null;
  STATE.signatureData = null;
  STATE.scoringResult = null;
  resetWizard();
  var pf = document.getElementById('previewFront'); if (pf) pf.style.display = 'none';
  var sfp = document.getElementById('scanFrontPlaceholder'); if (sfp) sfp.style.display = 'block';
  var ofr = document.getElementById('ocrFrontResult'); if (ofr) ofr.style.display = 'none';
  var b1 = document.getElementById('btnStep1Next'); if (b1) b1.disabled = true;
  var sf = document.getElementById('scannerFront'); if (sf) sf.classList.remove('captured');
  var pb = document.getElementById('previewBack'); if (pb) pb.style.display = 'none';
  var sbp = document.getElementById('scanBackPlaceholder'); if (sbp) sbp.style.display = 'block';
  var obr = document.getElementById('ocrBackResult'); if (obr) obr.style.display = 'none';
  var b2 = document.getElementById('btnStep2Next'); if (b2) b2.disabled = true;
  var sb = document.getElementById('scannerBack'); if (sb) sb.classList.remove('captured');
  for (var i = 1; i <= 6; i++) { var el = document.getElementById('step-' + i); if (el) el.style.display = (i === 1) ? 'block' : 'none'; }
  navTo('visita');
}

function resetWizard() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.wizard-step').forEach(function(el, i) {
    el.classList.remove('active', 'done');
    if (i === 0) el.classList.add('active');
  });
}

function goToStep(step) {
  if (step === 4) {
    var n = document.getElementById('formNombre').value.trim();
    var d = document.getElementById('formDni').value.trim();
    var t = document.getElementById('formTelefono').value.trim();
    if (!n || !d || !t) { showToast('Completa nombre, DNI y teléfono', 'error'); return; }
    STATE.kycData.nombre = n; STATE.kycData.dni = d; STATE.kycData.telefono = t;
    STATE.kycData.email = document.getElementById('formEmail').value;
    STATE.kycData.direccion = document.getElementById('formDireccion').value;
    STATE.kycData.poblacion = document.getElementById('formPoblacion').value;
    STATE.kycData.cp = document.getElementById('formCp').value;
    STATE.kycData.tipo = document.getElementById('formTipo').value;
    STATE.kycData.obs = document.getElementById('formObs').value;
  }
  STATE.currentStep = step;
  document.querySelectorAll('.wizard-step').forEach(function(el, i) {
    el.classList.remove('active', 'done');
    if (i < step - 1) el.classList.add('done');
    else if (i === step - 1) el.classList.add('active');
  });
  for (var i = 1; i <= 6; i++) { var el = document.getElementById('step-' + i); if (el) el.style.display = (i === step) ? 'block' : 'none'; }
  document.getElementById('appMain').scrollTop = 0;
  if (step === 4) runScoring();
  if (step === 6) fillContractData();
}

var _camStream = null;
var _camSide = 'front';
var _camFacing = 'environment';

function openCamera(side) {
  _camSide = side;
  document.getElementById('cameraFrameLabel').textContent = side === 'front' ? '📄 Coloca la cara FRONTAL del DNI/NIE' : '📄 Coloca la cara TRASERA del DNI/NIE';
  document.getElementById('cameraModal').classList.add('open');
  startStream();
}

function startStream() {
  if (_camStream) { _camStream.getTracks().forEach(function(t) { t.stop(); }); }
  navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: _camFacing, width: {ideal:1280}, height: {ideal:720} } })
    .then(function(stream) { _camStream = stream; document.getElementById('cameraVideo').srcObject = stream; })
    .catch(function() {
      closeCamera();
      document.getElementById(_camSide === 'front' ? 'fileFront' : 'fileBack').click();
      showToast('Abriendo galería (sin permiso de cámara)', 'warning');
    });
}

function switchCamera() { _camFacing = _camFacing === 'environment' ? 'user' : 'environment'; startStream(); }

function closeCamera() {
  if (_camStream) { _camStream.getTracks().forEach(function(t) { t.stop(); }); _camStream = null; }
  document.getElementById('cameraModal').classList.remove('open');
}

function captureFromCamera() {
  var video = document.getElementById('cameraVideo');
  var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = video.videoWidth || 1280;
  canvas.height = video.videoHeight || 720;
  canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(video, 0, 0);
  var dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.92);
  closeCamera();
  processCapture(_camSide, dataUrl, true);
}

function handleFileCapture(side, input) {
  var file = input.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  var reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) { processCapture(side, e.target.result, true); };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

var _tesseractWorker = null;
function getTesseractWorker() {
  if (_tesseractWorker) return Promise.resolve(_tesseractWorker);
  showToast('⏳ Cargando motor OCR…', 'warning');
  return Tesseract.createWorker('spa', 1, { logger: function(m) {
    if (m.status === 'recognizing text') {
      var pct = Math.round((m.progress || 0) * 100);
      var el = document.getElementById('ocrProgressBar');
      if (el) el.style.width = pct + '%';
    }
  }}).then(function(worker) { _tesseractWorker = worker; return worker; });
}

function parseFront(raw) {
  var text = raw.toUpperCase().replace(/[|]/g, 'I');
  var result = {};
  var dniMatch = text.match(/\b([XYZ]\d{7}[A-Z]|\d{8}[TRWAGMYFPDXBNJZSQVHLCKE])\b/);
  if (dniMatch) result.dni = dniMatch[1];
  var apellidosM = text.match(/APELLIDOS[:\s]+([A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑÜ ]+)/);
  var nombreM = text.match(/NOMBRE[:\s]+([A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑÜ ]+)/);
  if (apellidosM && nombreM) { result.nombre = nombreM[1].trim() + ' ' + apellidosM[1].trim(); }
  else {
    var mrzLines = text.match(/[A-Z<]{9,}/g);
    if (mrzLines && mrzLines.length >= 2) { var parts = mrzLines[1].replace(/<+/g, ' ').trim().split(' '); if (parts.length >= 2) result.nombre = parts.slice(1).join(' ') + ' ' + parts[0]; }
  }
  var fnacMatch = text.match(/\b(\d{2}[\s\/\-]\d{2}[\s\/\-]\d{2,4})\b/);
  if (fnacMatch) result.fechaNac = fnacMatch[1].replace(/\s+/g, '/');
  var sexoM = text.match(/\b(MASCULINO|FEMENINO|MALE|FEMALE|[MF])\b/);
  if (sexoM) result.sexo = sexoM[1].startsWith('M') ? 'M' : 'F';
  var cadM = Array.from(text.matchAll(/\b(\d{2}[\s\/]\d{2}[\s\/]\d{2,4})\b/g));
  if (cadM.length >= 2) result.caducidad = cadM[cadM.length - 1][1].slice(-4);
  return result;
}

function parseBack(raw) {
  var text = raw.toUpperCase();
  var result = {};
  var domM = text.match(/(?:DOMICILIO|DIRECCIÓN|CALLE|C\/)[ \s:]*([A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑÜ0-9\s,\.ºª]+)/);
  if (domM) result.domicilio = domM[1].trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').substring(0, 60);
  var munM = text.match(/(?:MUNICIPIO|LOCALIDAD|CIUDAD)[\s:]*([A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑÜ\s]+)/);
  if (munM) result.municipio = munM[1].trim().substring(0, 40);
  var lugarM = text.match(/(?:LUGAR DE NACIMIENTO|NACIMIENTO)[\s:]*([A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑÜ\s]+)/);
  if (lugarM) result.lugarNac = lugarM[1].trim().substring(0, 40);
  var canM = text.match(/\b(\d{6})\b/);
  if (canM) result.can = canM[1];
  return result;
}

function simulateScan(side) {
  navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: { ideal: 'environment' }, width: 1280, height: 720 } })
    .then(function(stream) { launchCameraCapture(side, stream); })
    .catch(function() { showToast('📁 Sin cámara. Selecciona una imagen del DNI.', 'warning'); launchFileCapture(side); });
}

function launchCameraCapture(side, stream) {
  var overlay = document.createElement('div');
  overlay.id = 'camOverlay';
  overlay.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%;background:#000;z-index:9999;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;';
  overlay.innerHTML = '<div style="position:relative;width:100%;max-width:480px;"><video id="camVideo" autoplay playsinline style="width:100%;border-radius:12px;"></video><div style="position:absolute;inset:10%;border:3px solid #f0c040;border-radius:10px;pointer-events:none;"></div></div><div style="color:#fff;font-size:13px;margin:12px 0;text-align:center;">Encuadra el ' + (side === 'front' ? 'FRENTE' : 'REVERSO') + ' del DNI<br>y pulsa <strong>Capturar</strong></div><div style="display:flex;gap:16px;"><button id="btnCapSim" style="background:#f0c040;color:#0a1628;border:none;padding:12px 28px;border-radius:10px;font-size:16px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">Capturar</button><button id="btnCanSim" style="background:#374151;color:#fff;border:none;padding:12px 28px;border-radius:10px;font-size:16px;cursor:pointer;">Cancelar</button></div>';
  document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  var video = document.getElementById('camVideo');
  video.srcObject = stream;
  document.getElementById('btnCapSim').onclick = function() {
    var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = video.videoWidth; canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
    canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(video, 0, 0);
    stream.getTracks().forEach(function(t) { t.stop(); });
    document.body.removeChild(overlay);
    processCapture(side, canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.92), true);
  };
  document.getElementById('btnCanSim').onclick = function() {
    stream.getTracks().forEach(function(t) { t.stop(); });
    document.body.removeChild(overlay);
  };
}

function launchFileCapture(side) {
  var inp = document.createElement('input');
  inp.type = 'file'; inp.accept = 'image/*';
  inp.onchange = function(e) {
    var file = e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    var reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = function(ev) { processCapture(side, ev.target.result, true); };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };
  inp.click();
}

function processCapture(side, dataUrl, runOcr) {
  showToast('🔍 Procesando imagen…', 'warning');
  if (side === 'front') {
    document.getElementById('previewFront').src = dataUrl;
    document.getElementById('previewFront').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('scanFrontPlaceholder').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('scannerFront').classList.add('captured');
    STATE.kycData.frontImage = dataUrl;
    if (runOcr) {
      getTesseractWorker().then(function(worker) { return worker.recognize(dataUrl); }).then(function(res) {
        var text = res.data.text;
        var loadDiv = document.getElementById('ocrLoadingFront'); if (loadDiv) loadDiv.style.display = 'none';
        var parsed = parseFront(text);
        STATE.kycData.nombre   = parsed.nombre   || STATE.kycData.nombre   || '(no detectado)';
        STATE.kycData.dni      = parsed.dni      || STATE.kycData.dni      || '';
        STATE.kycData.fechaNac = parsed.fechaNac || STATE.kycData.fechaNac || '';
        STATE.kycData.sexo     = parsed.sexo     || 'M';
        STATE.kycData.caducidad = parsed.caducidad || '2030';
        showOcrFront();
      }).catch(function() {
        showToast('⚠️ Error OCR; usando datos de ejemplo.', 'warning');
        STATE.kycData.nombre = STATE.kycData.nombre || 'Demo Usuario';
        STATE.kycData.dni    = STATE.kycData.dni    || '12345678A';
        showOcrFront();
      });
      var loadDiv = document.getElementById('ocrLoadingFront');
      if (loadDiv) loadDiv.style.display = 'block';
    } else { setTimeout(showOcrFront, 800); }
  } else {
    document.getElementById('previewBack').src = dataUrl;
    document.getElementById('previewBack').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('scanBackPlaceholder').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('scannerBack').classList.add('captured');
    STATE.kycData.backImage = dataUrl;
    if (runOcr) {
      getTesseractWorker().then(function(worker) { return worker.recognize(dataUrl); }).then(function(res) {
        var parsed = parseBack(res.data.text);
        STATE.kycData.domicilio = parsed.domicilio || 'Calle Mayor, 12 3º A';
        STATE.kycData.municipio = parsed.municipio || 'Xàtiva (Valencia)';
        STATE.kycData.lugarNac  = parsed.lugarNac  || 'Valencia';
        STATE.kycData.can       = parsed.can       || '';
        showOcrBack();
      }).catch(function() {
        showToast('⚠️ Error OCR; usando datos de ejemplo.', 'warning');
        STATE.kycData.domicilio = 'Calle Mayor, 12 3º A';
        STATE.kycData.municipio = 'Xàtiva (Valencia)';
        STATE.kycData.lugarNac  = 'Valencia';
        showOcrBack();
      });
    } else { setTimeout(showOcrBack, 800); }
  }
}

function showOcrFront() {
  document.getElementById('ocrNombre').textContent    = STATE.kycData.nombre   || '—';
  document.getElementById('ocrDni').textContent       = STATE.kycData.dni      || '—';
  document.getElementById('ocrFechaNac').textContent  = STATE.kycData.fechaNac || '—';
  document.getElementById('ocrSexo').textContent      = STATE.kycData.sexo === 'F' ? 'Femenino' : 'Masculino';
  document.getElementById('ocrCaducidad').textContent = STATE.kycData.caducidad ? '12/' + STATE.kycData.caducidad : '—';
  document.getElementById('ocrFrontResult').style.display = 'block';
  if (STATE.kycData.nombre) document.getElementById('formNombre').value = STATE.kycData.nombre;
  if (STATE.kycData.dni)    document.getElementById('formDni').value    = STATE.kycData.dni;
  document.getElementById('btnStep1Next').disabled = false;
  showToast('✓ Cara frontal reconocida', 'success');
}

function showOcrBack() {
  document.getElementById('ocrLugar').textContent     = STATE.kycData.lugarNac  || '—';
  document.getElementById('ocrDomicilio').textContent = STATE.kycData.domicilio  || '—';
  document.getElementById('ocrMunicipio').textContent = STATE.kycData.municipio  || '—';
  document.getElementById('ocrCan').textContent       = STATE.kycData.can        || '—';
  document.getElementById('ocrBackResult').style.display = 'block';
  if (STATE.kycData.domicilio) document.getElementById('formDireccion').value = STATE.kycData.domicilio;
  var pobMatch = (STATE.kycData.municipio || '').match(/([^(]+)/);
  if (pobMatch) document.getElementById('formPoblacion').value = pobMatch[1].trim();
  document.getElementById('formCp').value = '46800';
  document.getElementById('btnStep2Next').disabled = false;
  showToast('✓ Cara trasera reconocida', 'success');
}

function runScoring() {
  document.getElementById('scoringProcess').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('scoringResult').style.display = 'none';
  var dniValido = /^[0-9]{8}[A-Z]$|^[XYZ][0-9]{7}[A-Z]$/.test((STATE.kycData.dni || '').toUpperCase());
  var enListaNegra = checkBlacklist(STATE.kycData.dni);
  var pasaAsnef = Math.random() > 0.12;
  var checks = [
    { id:'chk1', label:'Validando formato DNI/NIE...', delay:700,  ok: dniValido },
    { id:'chk2', label:'Consultando base de datos CRM Seguxat...', delay:1200, ok: !enListaNegra },
    { id:'chk3', label:'Verificando lista negra interna...', delay:900,  ok: !enListaNegra },
    { id:'chk4', label:'Consultando ASNEF/Morosos...', delay:1400, ok: pasaAsnef },
    { id:'chk5', label:'Calculando scoring solvencia...', delay:1000, ok: true },
    { id:'chk6', label:'Generando decisión automática...', delay:600,  ok: true }
  ];
  var allOk = dniValido && !enListaNegra && pasaAsnef;
  var failedAt = null;
  var totalDelay = 0;
  checks.forEach(function(c, i) {
    totalDelay += c.delay;
    (function(check, idx, delay) {
      setTimeout(function() {
        var result = check.ok;
        if (!result && failedAt === null) { failedAt = idx; allOk = false; }
        var el = document.getElementById(check.id);
        el.style.opacity = '1';
        if (failedAt === idx) { el.className = 'check-item fail'; el.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> ' + check.label.replace('...','') + ': <strong>NO APTO</strong>'; }
        else if (failedAt === null || idx < failedAt) { el.className = 'check-item ok'; el.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> ' + check.label.replace('...','') + ': OK'; }
        if (i < checks.length - 1) {
          var next = document.getElementById(checks[idx + 1].id);
          if (next) { next.style.opacity = '1'; next.className = 'check-item checking'; next.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-circle-notch fa-spin"></i> ' + checks[idx + 1].label; }
        }
        if (idx === checks.length - 1) { setTimeout(function() { showScoringResult(allOk); }, 400); }
      }, delay);
    })(c, i, totalDelay);
  });
}

function checkBlacklist(dni) {
  var blacklist = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('seguxat_blacklist') || '[]');
  return blacklist.some(function(b) { return b.dni === dni; });
}

function showScoringResult(approved) {
  document.getElementById('scoringProcess').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('scoringResult').style.display = 'block';
  var score = approved ? (65 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30)) : (15 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 25));
  STATE.scoringResult = { approved: approved, score: score };
  var circle = document.getElementById('scoreCircle');
  var offset = 314 - (score / 100) * 314;
  var color = score >= 70 ? 'var(--green)' : (score >= 40 ? 'var(--orange)' : 'var(--red)');
  circle.style.stroke = color;
  setTimeout(function() { circle.style.strokeDashoffset = offset; }, 100);
  animateNumber('scoreNum', 0, score, 1500, color);
  var dec = document.getElementById('decisionBox');
  if (approved) {
    dec.innerHTML = '<div class="decision-box approved"><div class="decision-icon">✅</div><div class="decision-title" style="color:#166534">¡APROBADO!</div><div class="decision-desc" style="color:#166534">Score ' + score + '/100 · El cliente pasa todos los filtros.</div></div>';
    document.getElementById('btnScoringNext').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('btnScoringReject').style.display = 'none';
    showToast('✅ Cliente aprobado · Score: ' + score, 'success');
  } else {
    dec.innerHTML = '<div class="decision-box rejected"><div class="decision-icon">❌</div><div class="decision-title" style="color:#991b1b">NO APTO</div><div class="decision-desc" style="color:#991b1b">Score ' + score + '/100 · No procede contrato.</div></div>';
    document.getElementById('btnScoringNext').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('btnScoringReject').style.display = 'block';
    showToast('❌ Cliente no apto · Score: ' + score, 'error');
    saveVisita('rechazado');
  }
}

function cancelVisita() { navTo('inicio'); updateStats(); }

function compartirContratoWhatsapp() {
  var k = STATE.kycData;
  var kit = STATE.selectedKit;
  if (!kit) { showToast('Selecciona un kit primero', 'error'); return; }
  var tel = (k.telefono || '').replace(/\s/g, '');
  var msg = encodeURIComponent('🛡️ *Oferta Seguxat* para ' + (k.nombre || 'usted') + '\n\n📦 *' + kit.nombre + '*\n💶 Cuota: ' + kit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '€/mes\n🔧 Instalación: ' + kit.instalacion + '€\n📋 Permanencia: ' + kit.permanencia + '\n✅ CRA 24h homologada EN 50518 incluida\n\n📞 Más info: www.seguxat.com');
  var url = tel ? 'https://wa.me/34' + tel + '?text=' + msg : 'https://wa.me/?text=' + msg;
  window.open(url, '_blank');
}

function renderKitsGrid(filter) {
  var grid = document.getElementById('kitsGrid');
  if (!grid) return;
  var kits = filter ? KITS.filter(function(k) { return k.tipo === filter; }) : KITS;
  grid.innerHTML = kits.map(function(kit) { return buildKitCard(kit, true); }).join('');
  kits.forEach(function(kit) {
    var el = document.getElementById('kit-' + kit.id);
    if (el) el.addEventListener('click', function() { selectKit(kit.id); });
  });
}

function renderCatalogo(filter) {
  var grid = document.getElementById('catalogoGrid');
  if (!grid) return;
  var kits = filter ? KITS.filter(function(k) { return k.tipo === filter; }) : KITS;
  grid.innerHTML = kits.map(function(kit) { return buildKitCardCatalogo(kit); }).join('');
  kits.forEach(function(kit) {
    var el = document.getElementById('cat-' + kit.id);
    if (el) el.addEventListener('click', function() { openKitDetail(kit.id); });
    var shareBtn = document.getElementById('share-' + kit.id);
    if (shareBtn) shareBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); compartirKit(kit.id); });
  });
}

function buildKitCardCatalogo(kit) {
  var compIcons = {Central:'fa-microchip', Detector:'fa-radiation', Sensor:'fa-door-open', Sirena:'fa-volume-up', Camara:'fa-camera', App:'fa-mobile-alt', SMS:'fa-sms', Video:'fa-video'};
  function getIcon(comp) { var keys = Object.keys(compIcons); for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) { if (comp.indexOf(keys[i]) !== -1) return compIcons[keys[i]]; } return 'fa-check'; }
  var featuresHtml = kit.componentes.slice(0, 5).map(function(c) { return '<div class="kit-feature"><i class="fas ' + getIcon(c) + '" style="color:' + kit.color + ';font-size:0.6rem"></i>' + escHtml(c) + '</div>'; }).join('');
  if (kit.componentes.length > 5) featuresHtml += '<div class="kit-feature">+' + (kit.componentes.length - 5) + ' más</div>';
  return '<div class="kit-card" id="cat-' + kit.id + '">' +
    '<div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + kit.color + '18,' + kit.color + '30);padding:20px;text-align:center;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)">' +
    '<div style="font-size:3.5rem;margin-bottom:6px">' + kit.icon + '</div>' +
    '<div class="kit-name">' + escHtml(kit.nombre) + (kit.popular ? '&nbsp;<span class="badge badge-green" style="font-size:0.6rem">Popular</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
    '<div style="font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text2);margin-top:2px">' + escHtml(kit.descripcion) + '</div></div>' +
    '<div style="padding:14px">' +
    '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px">' +
    '<div><span style="font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--navy)">' + kit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '€</span><span style="font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text2)">/mes</span></div>' +
    '<div style="text-align:right"><div style="font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:var(--gold2)">' + kit.instalacion + '€</div><div style="font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text2)">instalación</div></div></div>' +
    '<div class="kit-features">' + featuresHtml + '</div>' +
    '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:12px">' +
    '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" id="share-' + kit.id + '" style="flex:1;font-size:0.72rem"><i class="fas fa-share-alt"></i> Compartir</button>' +
    '</div></div></div>';
}

function compartirKit(id) {
  var kit = KITS.find(function(k) { return k.id === id; });
  if (!kit) return;
  var txt = '🛡️ ' + kit.nombre + ' — Seguxat\n✅ Desde ' + kit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '€/mes\n📦 Instalación: ' + kit.instalacion + '€\n🏠 ' + kit.descripcion + '\n📞 www.seguxat.com';
  if (navigator.share) navigator.share({title: kit.nombre, text: txt});
  else if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(function() { showToast('Ficha copiada al portapapeles ✓', 'success'); });
}

function buildKitCard(kit, selectable) {
  var featuresHtml = kit.componentes.slice(0, 4).map(function(c) {
    return '<div class="kit-feature"><i class="fas fa-check" style="color:var(--green);font-size:0.6rem"></i>' + escHtml(c) + '</div>';
  }).join('');
  if (kit.componentes.length > 4) featuresHtml += '<div class="kit-feature">+' + (kit.componentes.length - 4) + ' más</div>';
  var checkHtml = selectable ? '<div class="kit-selected-check" id="check-' + kit.id + '" style="display:none"><i class="fas fa-check"></i></div>' : '';
  var tipoBadge = kit.tipo === 'hogar' ? '<span class="badge badge-blue">🏠 Hogar</span>' : (kit.tipo === 'comunidad' ? '<span class="badge badge-purple">🏘️ Comunidad</span>' : '<span class="badge badge-orange">🏢 Negocio</span>');
  return '<div class="kit-card" id="kit-' + kit.id + '">' +
    '<div class="kit-image-placeholder" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + kit.color + '22,' + kit.color + '44)"><span style="font-size:2.5rem">' + kit.icon + '</span></div>' +
    '<div class="kit-header"><div><div class="kit-name">' + escHtml(kit.nombre) + (kit.popular ? ' <span class="badge badge-green" style="font-size:0.6rem">Popular</span>' : '') + '</div><div style="font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text2);margin-top:2px">' + escHtml(kit.descripcion) + '</div></div>' + checkHtml + '</div>' +
    '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin:8px 0">' +
    '<div><div class="kit-price">' + kit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '€<span class="kit-cuota">/mes</span></div><div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text2)">Instalación: ' + kit.instalacion + '€</div></div>' +
    tipoBadge + '</div>' +
    '<div class="kit-features">' + featuresHtml + '</div></div>';
}

function selectKit(id) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.kit-card').forEach(function(c) { c.classList.remove('selected'); });
  document.querySelectorAll('[id^="check-"]').forEach(function(c) { c.style.display = 'none'; });
  STATE.selectedKit = KITS.find(function(k) { return k.id === id; });
  if (!STATE.selectedKit) return;
  var card = document.getElementById('kit-' + id);
  if (card) card.classList.add('selected');
  var chkEl = document.getElementById('check-' + id);
  if (chkEl) chkEl.style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('kitSeleccionado').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('kitSelNombre').textContent = STATE.selectedKit.nombre;
  document.getElementById('kitSelCuota').textContent = STATE.selectedKit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '€';
  document.getElementById('kitSelInstalacion').textContent = STATE.selectedKit.instalacion + '€';
  document.getElementById('btnKitNext').disabled = false;
  showToast('Kit seleccionado: ' + STATE.selectedKit.nombre, 'success');
}

function openKitDetail(id) {
  var kit = KITS.find(function(k) { return k.id === id; });
  if (!kit) return;
  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = kit.icon + ' ' + kit.nombre;
  document.getElementById('modalDesc').textContent = kit.descripcion;
  document.getElementById('modalContent').innerHTML = '<div style="margin-bottom:12px"><strong>' + kit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '€/mes</strong> · Instalación: ' + kit.instalacion + '€ · Permanencia: ' + kit.permanencia + '</div><div>' + kit.componentes.map(function(c) { return '<div style="padding:4px 0;font-size:0.82rem;">✓ ' + escHtml(c) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div><button class="btn btn-gold btn-full" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="closeModal();compartirKit(\'' + id + '\');"><i class="fas fa-share-alt"></i> Compartir kit</button>';
  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('open');
}

function fillContractData() {
  var k = STATE.kycData;
  var kit = STATE.selectedKit;
  document.getElementById('cnNombre').textContent = k.nombre || '–';
  document.getElementById('cnDni').textContent    = k.dni    || '–';
  document.getElementById('cnTel').textContent    = k.telefono || '–';
  document.getElementById('cnDir').textContent    = (k.direccion || '–') + (k.poblacion ? ', ' + k.poblacion : '');
  document.getElementById('cnKit').textContent    = kit ? kit.nombre : '–';
  document.getElementById('cnCuota').textContent  = kit ? kit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '€/mes' : '–';
  document.getElementById('cnInst').textContent   = kit ? kit.instalacion + '€ (pago único)' : '–';
  document.getElementById('cnPerm').textContent   = kit ? kit.permanencia : '–';
}

function checkContractReady() {
  var rgpd  = document.getElementById('checkRgpd').checked;
  var hasSig = STATE.signatureData !== null;
  document.getElementById('btnFinalizar').disabled = !(rgpd && hasSig);
}

function finalizarContrato() {
  if (!document.getElementById('checkRgpd').checked) { showToast('Acepta las condiciones primero', 'error'); return; }
  if (!STATE.signatureData) { showToast('El cliente debe firmar primero', 'error'); return; }
  showLoading('Enviando contrato a CRM...', 'Registrando firma digital y notificando CRA');
  setTimeout(function() {
    hideLoading();
    saveVisita('aprobado');
    updateStats();
    renderVisitas();
    showToast('✅ Contrato firmado y enviado · ' + (STATE.kycData.nombre || ''), 'success');
    setTimeout(function() { navTo('inicio'); }, 1800);
  }, 2200);
}

function saveVisita(estado) {
  var visita = {
    id: Date.now(),
    nombre: STATE.kycData.nombre || 'Desconocido',
    dni: STATE.kycData.dni || '',
    telefono: STATE.kycData.telefono || '',
    direccion: STATE.kycData.direccion || '',
    kit: STATE.selectedKit ? STATE.selectedKit.nombre : '–',
    kitId: STATE.selectedKit ? STATE.selectedKit.id : null,
    cuota: STATE.selectedKit ? STATE.selectedKit.cuotaMes : 0,
    instalacion: STATE.selectedKit ? STATE.selectedKit.instalacion : 0,
    score: STATE.scoringResult ? STATE.scoringResult.score : 0,
    estado: estado,
    fecha: new Date().toISOString(),
    hora: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('es-ES', {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'}),
    comercial: STATE.comercial.nombre
  };
  STATE.visitas.push(visita);
  localStorage.setItem('seguxat_visitas', JSON.stringify(STATE.visitas));
  return visita;
}

function initSignature() {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('sigCanvas');
  if (!canvas) return;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  var drawing = false;
  function resizeCanvas() { var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); canvas.width = rect.width || 320; canvas.height = 160; }
  resizeCanvas();
  window.addEventListener('resize', resizeCanvas);
  function getPos(e) {
    var r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (e.touches && e.touches.length) return { x: e.touches[0].clientX - r.left, y: e.touches[0].clientY - r.top };
    return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
  }
  function startDraw(e) { e.preventDefault(); drawing = true; var p = getPos(e); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y); }
  function draw(e) {
    if (!drawing) return; e.preventDefault();
    var p = getPos(e);
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#0a1628';
    ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.stroke();
    STATE.signatureData = canvas.toDataURL();
    document.getElementById('sigStatus').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check-circle" style="color:var(--green)"></i> Firma registrada';
    checkContractReady();
  }
  function endDraw() { drawing = false; }
  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', startDraw);
  canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', draw);
  canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', endDraw);
  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', startDraw, {passive:false});
  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', draw, {passive:false});
  canvas.addEventListener('touchend', endDraw);
}

function clearSignature() {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('sigCanvas');
  if (!canvas) return;
  canvas.getContext('2d').clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  STATE.signatureData = null;
  document.getElementById('sigStatus').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-pen-nib" style="opacity:0.4"></i> Esperando firma...';
  checkContractReady();
}

function calcularSim() {
  var kitId = document.getElementById('simKit').value;
  var kit = KITS.find(function(k) { return k.id === kitId; }) || KITS[0];
  var permanencia = parseInt(document.getElementById('simPermanencia').value);
  var descuento = parseInt(document.getElementById('simDescuento').value);
  var financiacion = document.getElementById('simFinanciacion').value;
  var cuotaBase = kit.cuotaMes;
  var cuotaDesc = cuotaBase * (1 - descuento / 100);
  var instalacion = (permanencia >= 36 && financiacion === '0-incluida') ? 0 : kit.instalacion;
  var cuotaFinal = cuotaDesc;
  if (financiacion !== '0' && financiacion !== '0-incluida') { cuotaFinal = cuotaDesc + (kit.instalacion / parseInt(financiacion)); }
  var totalContrato = (cuotaFinal * permanencia) + (financiacion === '0' ? instalacion : 0);
  var ahorro = Math.round((kit.pvpRef * permanencia) - totalContrato);
  var compRef = kit.pvpRef + 5;
  document.getElementById('simCuotaMes').textContent = cuotaFinal.toFixed(2) + '€';
  document.getElementById('simInstalacion').textContent = instalacion === 0 ? 'Gratis 🎁' : instalacion + '€';
  document.getElementById('simTotalContrato').textContent = totalContrato.toFixed(0) + '€';
  document.getElementById('simAhorro').textContent = ahorro > 0 ? ahorro + '€ 💰' : '–';
  document.getElementById('simComparativa').textContent = 'Securitas Direct: ~' + compRef.toFixed(2) + '€/mes · Ahorro Seguxat: ' + (compRef - cuotaFinal).toFixed(2) + '€/mes';
}

function shareOffer() {
  var kitId = document.getElementById('simKit').value;
  var kit = KITS.find(function(k) { return k.id === kitId; }) || KITS[0];
  var cuota = document.getElementById('simCuotaMes').textContent;
  var text = '🛡️ Oferta Seguxat - ' + kit.nombre + '\n📦 Desde ' + cuota + '/mes\n🏠 Instalación profesional incluida\n📞 Contacta: www.seguxat.com';
  if (navigator.share) { navigator.share({ title: 'Oferta Seguxat', text: text }); }
  else if (navigator.clipboard) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function() { showToast('Oferta copiada al portapapeles', 'success'); }); }
}

function renderVisitas() {
  var list = document.getElementById('visitasList');
  if (!list) return;
  var visitas = STATE.visitas.slice().reverse();
  if (STATE.currentFilter !== 'all') {
    visitas = visitas.filter(function(v) {
      if (STATE.currentFilter === 'approved') return v.estado === 'aprobado';
      if (STATE.currentFilter === 'rejected') return v.estado === 'rechazado';
      if (STATE.currentFilter === 'pending')  return v.estado === 'pendiente';
      return true;
    });
  }
  document.getElementById('visitasCount').textContent = visitas.length + ' visita' + (visitas.length !== 1 ? 's' : '');
  if (!visitas.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text2);font-size:0.82rem;padding:40px 0"><i class="fas fa-search" style="font-size:2rem;opacity:0.3;display:block;margin-bottom:8px"></i>No hay visitas con este filtro</div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = visitas.map(function(v) {
    var bgColor = v.estado === 'aprobado' ? 'linear-gradient(135deg,#059669,#10b981)' : (v.estado === 'rechazado' ? 'linear-gradient(135deg,#dc2626,#ef4444)' : 'linear-gradient(135deg,#d97706,#f59e0b)');
    var badgeCls = v.estado === 'aprobado' ? 'badge-green' : (v.estado === 'rechazado' ? 'badge-red' : 'badge-orange');
    var badgeTxt = v.estado === 'aprobado' ? 'Aprobado' : (v.estado === 'rechazado' ? 'Rechazado' : 'Pendiente');
    var extraHtml = v.estado === 'aprobado' ? '<div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--gold2);font-weight:600;margin-top:4px">' + (v.cuota ? v.cuota.toFixed(2) : '0') + '€/m</div>' : '';
    return '<div class="list-item"><div class="list-avatar" style="background:' + bgColor + '"><i class="fas fa-user" style="color:#fff"></i></div><div class="list-info"><div class="list-name">' + escHtml(v.nombre) + '</div><div class="list-sub">' + escHtml(v.kit) + ' · Score ' + v.score + '</div><div class="list-sub">' + new Date(v.fecha).toLocaleDateString('es-ES') + ' ' + v.hora + '</div></div><div class="list-meta"><span class="badge ' + badgeCls + '">' + badgeTxt + '</span>' + extraHtml + '</div></div>';
  }).join('');
}

function showToast(msg, type) {
  var t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.className = 'toast' + (type === 'success' ? ' success' : type === 'error' ? ' error' : type === 'warning' ? ' warning' : '');
  t.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(function() { t.classList.remove('show'); }, 3000);
}

function showLoading(title, sub) {
  document.getElementById('loadingTitle').textContent = title || 'Cargando...';
  document.getElementById('loadingSub').textContent = sub || '';
  document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').classList.add('show');
}

function hideLoading() { document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').classList.remove('show'); }
function closeModal() { document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.remove('open'); }

function animateNumber(id, from, to, duration, color) {
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!el) return;
  el.style.color = color;
  var start = performance.now();
  function update(now) {
    var p = Math.min((now - start) / duration, 1);
    el.textContent = Math.round(from + (to - from) * p);
    if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(update);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(update);
}

function logout() { if (confirm('¿Cerrar sesión?')) { window.location.href = '../login.html'; } }

var _presIndex = 0;
var _presKits = [];
var _presForVisita = false;

function openPresentacion(forVisita) {
  _presForVisita = !!forVisita;
  _presKits = KITS;
  _presIndex = 0;
  renderPresSlide();
  renderPresDots();
  document.getElementById('presOverlay').classList.add('open');
}

function closePresentacion() { document.getElementById('presOverlay').classList.remove('open'); }
function presNav(dir) { _presIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(_presKits.length - 1, _presIndex + dir)); renderPresSlide(); renderPresDots(); }

function renderPresDots() {
  var cont = document.getElementById('presDots');
  cont.innerHTML = _presKits.map(function(_, i) {
    return '<div class="pres-dot' + (i === _presIndex ? ' active' : '') + '" data-pres-idx="' + i + '"></div>';
  }).join('');
  cont.querySelectorAll('.pres-dot').forEach(function(dot) {
    dot.addEventListener('click', function() { _presIndex = parseInt(this.getAttribute('data-pres-idx')); renderPresSlide(); renderPresDots(); });
  });
  document.getElementById('presKitCounter').textContent = (_presIndex + 1) + ' / ' + _presKits.length;
  document.getElementById('presBtnPrev').style.opacity = _presIndex === 0 ? '0.3' : '1';
  var isLast = _presIndex === _presKits.length - 1;
  document.getElementById('presBtnNext').style.display = isLast ? 'none' : 'block';
  document.getElementById('presBtnSelect').style.display = _presForVisita ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function renderPresSlide() {
  var kit = _presKits[_presIndex];
  var rival = (kit.pvpRef + 5).toFixed(2);
  var ahorro = ((kit.pvpRef - kit.cuotaMes) * 12).toFixed(0);
  var featsHtml = kit.componentes.map(function(c) { return '<div class="pres-feat"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i><span>' + escHtml(c) + '</span></div>'; }).join('');
  document.getElementById('presBody').innerHTML =
    '<div class="pres-kit-hero"><span class="pres-icon">' + kit.icon + '</span><div class="pres-name">' + escHtml(kit.nombre) + '</div><div class="pres-desc">' + escHtml(kit.descripcion) + '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="pres-price-box"><div class="pres-price-main">' + kit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '<span style="font-size:1.4rem">€</span></div><div class="pres-price-sub">al mes · sin sorpresas</div><div class="pres-price-inst">Instalación profesional: <strong style="color:#f0c040">' + kit.instalacion + '€</strong></div><div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:0.8rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.5)">Permanencia: ' + kit.permanencia + '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="pres-features-grid">' + featsHtml + '</div>' +
    '<div class="pres-compare"><div style="font-size:0.75rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.45);margin-bottom:8px">Comparativa de precio</div><div class="pres-compare-row"><span class="lbl">🛡️ Seguxat</span><span class="seguxat">' + kit.cuotaMes.toFixed(2) + '€/mes</span></div><div class="pres-compare-row"><span class="lbl">🔴 Securitas Direct</span><span class="rival">~' + rival + '€/mes</span></div><div class="pres-compare-row"><span class="lbl">💰 Ahorro anual</span><span class="seguxat">+' + ahorro + '€ al año</span></div></div>';
}

function presSelectCurrent() {
  var kit = _presKits[_presIndex];
  selectKit(kit.id);
  closePresentacion();
  navTo('visita');
  showToast('Kit seleccionado: ' + kit.nombre, 'success');
}

}

  if (!str) return '';
  var d = document.createElement('div'); d.textContent = str; return d.innerHTML;
}

function doLogin() {
  var u = document.getElementById('loginUser').value;
  if (!u) { toast('Introduce usuario', 'warn'); return; }
  quickLogin('Paco Martínez', 'I01', 'PM');
}

function quickLogin(nombre, id, av) {
  STATE.instaladorId = id;
  STATE.instaladorNombre = nombre;
  STATE.instaladorAv = av;
  document.getElementById('loginScreen').style.display = 'none';
  var shell = document.getElementById('appShell');
  shell.style.display = 'flex';
  var fab = document.getElementById('fabNuevaOT');
  fab.style.display = 'flex';
  initApp();
}

function doLogout() {
  document.getElementById('appShell').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('fabNuevaOT').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('loginScreen').style.display = 'flex';
}

function initApp() {
  var ins = INSTALADORES[STATE.instaladorId] || INSTALADORES['I01'];
  document.getElementById('hAvatar').textContent = ins.av;
  document.getElementById('hName').textContent = ins.nombre;
  var hoy = new Date();
  document.getElementById('hDate').textContent = hoy.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {weekday:'short', day:'numeric', month:'short'});
  document.getElementById('dashWelcome').textContent = saludo() + ' ' + ins.nombre.split(' ')[0] + ' 👋';
  document.getElementById('dashSub').textContent = 'Hoy, ' + hoy.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {weekday:'long', day:'numeric', month:'long'});
  document.getElementById('perfilAv').textContent = ins.av;
  document.getElementById('perfilName').textContent = ins.nombre;
  STATE.selectedDay = hoy;
  cargarOrdenes();
  initNotifs();
  buildWeekDays();
}

function saludo() {
  var h = new Date().getHours();
  return h < 12 ? 'Buenos días' : (h < 20 ? 'Buenas tardes' : 'Buenas noches');
}

function cargarOrdenes() {
  STATE.ordenes = ORDENES_DEMO;
  renderDash(); renderOrdenes(); renderAgenda(); renderPerfil();
}

function renderDash() {
  var mis = STATE.ordenes.filter(function(o) { return o.instalador_id === STATE.instaladorId; });
  var hoy = new Date().toDateString();
  var pendientes = mis.filter(function(o) { return ['programada','en_curso','pendiente'].indexOf(o.estado) !== -1; }).length;
  var deHoy     = mis.filter(function(o) { return o.fecha_programada && new Date(o.fecha_programada).toDateString() === hoy; }).length;
  var completadas = mis.filter(function(o) { return o.estado === 'completada'; }).length;
  s('dkPend', pendientes); s('dkHoy', deHoy); s('dkOk', completadas);
  var badge = document.getElementById('navBadgeOrd');
  if (pendientes > 0) { badge.style.display = 'flex'; badge.textContent = pendientes; } else badge.style.display = 'none';
  var proximas = mis.filter(function(o) { return o.estado !== 'completada' && o.estado !== 'cancelada'; })
    .sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(a.fecha_programada || 0) - new Date(b.fecha_programada || 0); })
    .slice(0, 3);
  var cont = document.getElementById('dashOrdenes');
  if (!proximas.length) { cont.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><i class="fas fa-check-circle" style="color:var(--green)"></i><p>¡Sin órdenes pendientes!</p></div>'; return; }
  cont.innerHTML = proximas.map(function(o) { return buildOrdenCard(o); }).join('');
  proximas.forEach(function(o) {
    var el = document.getElementById('orden-card-' + o.id);
    if (el) el.addEventListener('click', function() { abrirDetalle(o.id); });
  });
}

function renderOrdenes() {
  var q = document.getElementById('ordenSearch').value.toLowerCase();
  var f = document.getElementById('ordenFiltro').value;
  var mis = STATE.ordenes.filter(function(o) { return o.instalador_id === STATE.instaladorId; });
  var data = mis.filter(function(o) {
    if (f && o.estado !== f) return false;
    if (q && ![o.cliente_nombre, o.direccion, o.numero_ot, o.kit_nombre].some(function(v) { return (v || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(q) !== -1; })) return false;
    return true;
  }).sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(a.fecha_programada || 0) - new Date(b.fecha_programada || 0); });
  var cont = document.getElementById('ordenesLista');
  if (!data.length) { cont.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><i class="fas fa-search"></i><p>Sin órdenes con ese filtro</p></div>'; return; }
  cont.innerHTML = data.map(function(o) { return buildOrdenCard(o); }).join('');
  data.forEach(function(o) {
    var el = document.getElementById('orden-card-' + o.id);
    if (el) el.addEventListener('click', function() { abrirDetalle(o.id); });
  });
}

function filtrarOrdenes() { renderOrdenes(); }

function buildOrdenCard(o) {
  var estadoCls = ECLASS[o.estado] || 'e-pendiente';
  var eLabel    = ELBL[o.estado]   || o.estado;
  var prioCls   = o.prioridad === 'urgente' ? 'prio-urgente' : 'prio-normal';
  var prioLabel = o.prioridad === 'urgente' ? '🔴 URGENTE' : 'Normal';
  var fecha = o.fecha_programada ? new Date(o.fecha_programada).toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {weekday:'short', day:'numeric', month:'short', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'}) : 'Sin fecha';
  return '<div class="orden-card ' + (o.prioridad === 'urgente' ? 'urgente' : 'normal') + '" id="orden-card-' + o.id + '">' +
    '<div class="orden-num">' + escIns(o.numero_ot || o.id) + '</div>' +
    '<div class="orden-cliente">' + escIns(o.cliente_nombre || '—') + '</div>' +
    '<div class="orden-kit"><i class="fas fa-shield-alt" style="margin-right:4px;color:var(--text2)"></i>' + escIns(o.kit_nombre || '—') + ' · ' + escIns(o.direccion || '—') + '</div>' +
    '<div class="orden-meta"><span class="estado-badge ' + estadoCls + '">' + eLabel + '</span><span class="orden-fecha"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i>' + fecha + '</span><span class="orden-prio ' + prioCls + '">' + prioLabel + '</span></div></div>';
}

function buildWeekDays() {
  var hoy = new Date();
  var dias = [];
  for (var i = -1; i <= 5; i++) { var d = new Date(hoy); d.setDate(hoy.getDate() + i); dias.push(d); }
  var cont = document.getElementById('weekDays');
  cont.innerHTML = dias.map(function(d, idx) {
    var isToday = d.toDateString() === hoy.toDateString();
    var isSel   = STATE.selectedDay && d.toDateString() === STATE.selectedDay.toDateString();
    var bg = isSel ? 'background:var(--navy);color:#fff;' : (isToday ? 'background:var(--gold)18;border:2px solid var(--gold);' : 'border:2px solid transparent;');
    var clrLabel = isSel ? 'color:rgba(255,255,255,.6)' : 'color:var(--text2)';
    var clrNum   = isSel ? 'color:#fff' : 'color:var(--text)';
    return '<div data-day-idx="' + idx + '" data-day-iso="' + d.toISOString() + '" style="flex-shrink:0;width:48px;text-align:center;padding:8px 4px;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .2s;' + bg + '">' +
      '<div style="font-size:.65rem;font-weight:600;' + clrLabel + ';text-transform:uppercase">' + d.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {weekday:'short'}) + '</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:1rem;font-weight:800;' + clrNum + ';margin-top:2px">' + d.getDate() + '</div>' +
      '<div style="height:6px"></div></div>';
  }).join('');
  cont.querySelectorAll('[data-day-iso]').forEach(function(el) {
    el.addEventListener('click', function() { STATE.selectedDay = new Date(this.getAttribute('data-day-iso')); buildWeekDays(); renderAgenda(); });
  });
}

function renderAgenda() {
  var dia = STATE.selectedDay || new Date();
  var mis = STATE.ordenes.filter(function(o) {
    return o.instalador_id === STATE.instaladorId && o.fecha_programada && new Date(o.fecha_programada).toDateString() === dia.toDateString();
  });
  var cont = document.getElementById('agendaContent');
  if (!mis.length) { cont.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state" style="padding:60px 24px"><i class="fas fa-calendar-check"></i><p>Sin órdenes programadas para este día</p></div>'; return; }
  mis.sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(a.fecha_programada) - new Date(b.fecha_programada); });
  cont.innerHTML = mis.map(function(o) {
    var h = o.fecha_programada ? new Date(o.fecha_programada).toLocaleTimeString('es-ES', {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'}) : '—';
    var barCol = o.estado === 'completada' ? 'var(--green)' : (o.prioridad === 'urgente' ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--blue)');
    return '<div class="agenda-slot" id="agenda-slot-' + o.id + '">' +
      '<div class="slot-time"><div class="slot-h">' + h + '</div><div class="slot-dur">~2h</div></div>' +
      '<div class="slot-bar" style="background:' + barCol + '"></div>' +
      '<div class="slot-info"><div class="slot-cliente">' + escIns(o.cliente_nombre || '—') + '</div><div class="slot-kit">' + escIns(o.kit_nombre || '—') + '</div><div class="slot-addr"><i class="fas fa-map-marker-alt"></i>' + escIns(o.direccion || '—') + ', ' + escIns(o.poblacion || '') + '</div></div></div>';
  }).join('');
  mis.forEach(function(o) {
    var el = document.getElementById('agenda-slot-' + o.id);
    if (el) el.addEventListener('click', function() { abrirDetalle(o.id); });
  });
}

function abrirDetalle(id) {
  var o = null;
  for (var i = 0; i < STATE.ordenes.length; i++) { if (STATE.ordenes[i].id === id) { o = STATE.ordenes[i]; break; } }
  if (!o) return;
  STATE.currentOrden = o;
  STATE.checklistState = JSON.parse(o.checklist_json || '{}');
  STATE.valoracion = o.valoracion || 0;
  STATE.firmaData = o.firma_cliente || null;
  document.getElementById('dTitle').textContent = o.numero_ot || o.id;
  var badge = document.getElementById('dBadge');
  badge.textContent = ELBL[o.estado] || o.estado;
  badge.className = 'detail-hbadge ' + (ECLASS[o.estado] || 'e-pendiente');
  renderDetalleBody(o);
  document.getElementById('detalleOverlay').classList.add('open');
}

function cerrarDetalle() { document.getElementById('detalleOverlay').classList.remove('open'); }

function renderDetalleBody(o) {
  var kClTotal = CHECKLIST_DEF.reduce(function(acc, sec) { return acc + sec.items.length; }, 0);
  var kClDone  = Object.values(STATE.checklistState).filter(function(v) { return v === 'ok'; }).length;
  var pct = Math.round(kClDone / kClTotal * 100);
  var actionBtns = '';
  if (o.estado === 'programada') actionBtns += '<button class="action-btn ab-start" data-action="iniciar"><i class="fas fa-play"></i> Iniciar trabajo</button>';
  if (o.estado === 'en_curso')   actionBtns += '<button class="action-btn ab-complete" data-action="completar"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> Completar</button>';
  if (['programada','en_curso'].indexOf(o.estado) !== -1) actionBtns += '<button class="action-btn ab-incidencia" data-action="incidencia"><i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Incidencia</button>';
  actionBtns += '<button class="action-btn ab-nav" data-action="mapa"><i class="fas fa-map-marked-alt"></i> Navegar</button>';
  actionBtns += '<button class="action-btn ab-checklist" data-action="checklist"><i class="fas fa-tasks"></i> Checklist (' + kClDone + '/' + kClTotal + ')</button>';
  var llamarHtml = (o.estado !== 'completada' && o.cliente_telefono) ? '<button class="action-btn" style="grid-column:span 2;background:#fff;border:1.5px solid var(--border);color:var(--text);font-size:.75rem" data-action="llamar"><i class="fas fa-phone" style="color:var(--green)"></i> Llamar cliente</button>' : '';
  document.getElementById('detalleBody').innerHTML =
    '<div style="margin-bottom:14px"><div class="action-btns">' + actionBtns + llamarHtml + '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="cl-progress"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:.78rem;font-weight:600"><span>Checklist instalación</span><span style="color:' + (pct === 100 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--text2)') + '">' + kClDone + '/' + kClTotal + ' pasos (' + pct + '%)</span></div><div class="cl-prog-bar"><div class="cl-prog-fill" style="width:' + pct + '%"></div></div></div>' +
    '<div class="card"><div class="card-head"><h3><i class="fas fa-user" style="color:var(--gold)"></i> Cliente</h3></div><div class="card-body"><div class="detail-row"><span class="dr-lbl">Nombre</span><span class="dr-val">' + escIns(o.cliente_nombre || '—') + '</span></div><div class="detail-row"><span class="dr-lbl">Teléfono</span><span class="dr-val"><a href="tel:' + (o.cliente_telefono||'') + '" style="color:var(--blue)">' + (o.cliente_telefono||'—') + '</a></span></div><div class="detail-row"><span class="dr-lbl">Dirección</span><span class="dr-val" style="max-width:180px">' + escIns(o.direccion || '—') + '</span></div></div></div>' +
    '<div class="card"><div class="card-head"><h3><i class="fas fa-shield-alt" style="color:var(--blue)"></i> Kit a instalar</h3></div><div class="card-body"><div class="detail-row"><span class="dr-lbl">Kit</span><span class="dr-val">' + escIns(o.kit_nombre || '—') + '</span></div><div class="detail-row"><span class="dr-lbl">Fecha prog.</span><span class="dr-val">' + fd(o.fecha_programada) + '</span></div></div></div>' +
    '<div class="card"><div class="card-head"><h3><i class="fas fa-sticky-note" style="color:var(--purple)"></i> Notas del instalador</h3></div><div class="card-body"><textarea class="finput" id="notasInput" placeholder="Añade observaciones sobre la instalación...">' + escIns(o.notas_instalador || '') + '</textarea><button class="action-btn" style="width:100%;background:var(--navy);color:#fff;margin-top:8px;border-radius:9px;font-size:.78rem" id="btnGuardarNotas"><i class="fas fa-save"></i> Guardar notas</button></div></div>' +
    '<div style="height:16px"></div>';
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-action]').forEach(function(btn) {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
      var action = this.getAttribute('data-action');
      if (action === 'iniciar') cambiarEstado(o.id, 'en_curso');
      else if (action === 'completar') confirmarCompletar(o.id);
      else if (action === 'incidencia') reportarIncidencia(o.id);
      else if (action === 'mapa') abrirMapa(o.direccion, o.poblacion);
      else if (action === 'checklist') irChecklist();
      else if (action === 'llamar') llamarCliente(o.cliente_telefono);
    });
  });
  var btnGuardar = document.getElementById('btnGuardarNotas');
  if (btnGuardar) btnGuardar.addEventListener('click', function() { guardarNotas(o.id); });
  initCanvas();
}

function guardarNotas(id) {
  var notas = (document.getElementById('notasInput') || {}).value || '';
  var idx = STATE.ordenes.findIndex(function(o) { return o.id === id; });
  if (idx >= 0) STATE.ordenes[idx].notas_instalador = notas;
  toast('Notas guardadas ✅', 'ok');
}

function irChecklist() {
  var o = STATE.currentOrden;
  var body = document.getElementById('detalleBody');
  body.innerHTML = '<button id="btnVolverDetalle" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;background:none;border:none;color:var(--blue);font-size:.82rem;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;margin-bottom:14px;padding:4px 0"><i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Volver al detalle</button>' +
    '<div class="cl-progress"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:.78rem;font-weight:600" id="clProgLbl"><span>Progreso checklist</span><span>Calculando...</span></div><div class="cl-prog-bar"><div class="cl-prog-fill" id="clProgFill" style="width:0%"></div></div></div>' +
    '<div class="checklist-wrap" id="clWrap">' +
    CHECKLIST_DEF.map(function(sec) {
      return '<div class="cl-section"><h4><i class="fas fa-chevron-right" style="color:var(--gold)"></i> ' + sec.sec + '</h4>' +
        sec.items.map(function(item) {
          var st = STATE.checklistState[item.id] || 'pending';
          var cls = st === 'ok' ? 'done' : (st === 'fail' ? 'fail' : '');
          var chkContent = st === 'ok' ? '<i class="fas fa-check"></i>' : (st === 'fail' ? '<i class="fas fa-times"></i>' : '');
          return '<div class="cl-item ' + cls + '" id="cli_' + item.id + '">' +
            '<div class="cl-check">' + chkContent + '</div>' +
            '<div class="cl-text"><div class="cl-lbl">' + escIns(item.lbl) + '</div><div class="cl-desc">' + escIns(item.desc) + '</div></div>' +
            '<div class="cl-actions"><button class="cl-btn ok" data-item="' + item.id + '" data-val="ok"><i class="fas fa-check"></i></button><button class="cl-btn ko" data-item="' + item.id + '" data-val="fail"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button></div></div>';
        }).join('') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>' +
    '<div style="padding:0 16px;margin-top:4px"><button class="action-btn ab-start" style="width:100%" id="btnGuardarCL"><i class="fas fa-save"></i> Guardar checklist</button></div><div style="height:20px"></div>';
  document.getElementById('btnVolverDetalle').addEventListener('click', function() { renderDetalleBody(STATE.currentOrden); });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-item][data-val]').forEach(function(btn) {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function() { toggleCheck(this.getAttribute('data-item'), this.getAttribute('data-val')); });
  });
  document.getElementById('btnGuardarCL').addEventListener('click', function() { guardarChecklist(o.id); });
  updateClProgress();
}

function toggleCheck(id, val) {
  STATE.checklistState[id] = STATE.checklistState[id] === val ? 'pending' : val;
  var el = document.getElementById('cli_' + id);
  if (el) {
    el.className = 'cl-item' + (STATE.checklistState[id] === 'ok' ? ' done' : (STATE.checklistState[id] === 'fail' ? ' fail' : ''));
    var chk = el.querySelector('.cl-check');
    if (chk) chk.innerHTML = STATE.checklistState[id] === 'ok' ? '<i class="fas fa-check"></i>' : (STATE.checklistState[id] === 'fail' ? '<i class="fas fa-times"></i>' : '');
  }
  updateClProgress();
}

function updateClProgress() {
  var total = CHECKLIST_DEF.reduce(function(acc, s) { return acc + s.items.length; }, 0);
  var done  = Object.values(STATE.checklistState).filter(function(v) { return v === 'ok'; }).length;
  var pct   = Math.round(done / total * 100);
  var fill  = document.getElementById('clProgFill');
  var lbl   = document.getElementById('clProgLbl');
  if (fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
  if (lbl) { var spans = lbl.querySelectorAll('span'); if (spans[1]) spans[1].textContent = done + '/' + total + ' (' + pct + '%)'; }
}

function guardarChecklist(id) {
  var idx = STATE.ordenes.findIndex(function(o) { return o.id === id; });
  if (idx >= 0) STATE.ordenes[idx].checklist_json = JSON.stringify(STATE.checklistState);
  toast('Checklist guardado ✅', 'ok');
}

function cambiarEstado(id, nuevoEstado) {
  var extra = {};
  if (nuevoEstado === 'en_curso') extra.hora_inicio = new Date().toISOString();
  var idx = STATE.ordenes.findIndex(function(o) { return o.id === id; });
  if (idx >= 0) STATE.ordenes[idx] = Object.assign({}, STATE.ordenes[idx], {estado: nuevoEstado}, extra);
  STATE.currentOrden = idx >= 0 ? STATE.ordenes[idx] : STATE.currentOrden;
  var badge = document.getElementById('dBadge');
  badge.textContent = ELBL[nuevoEstado] || nuevoEstado;
  badge.className   = 'detail-hbadge ' + (ECLASS[nuevoEstado] || 'e-pendiente');
  renderDetalleBody(STATE.currentOrden);
  renderDash(); renderOrdenes();
  toast(nuevoEstado === 'en_curso' ? 'Trabajo iniciado ✅' : 'Estado actualizado ✅', 'ok');
}

function confirmarCompletar(id) {
  var total = CHECKLIST_DEF.reduce(function(acc, s) { return acc + s.items.length; }, 0);
  var done  = Object.values(STATE.checklistState).filter(function(v) { return v === 'ok'; }).length;
  if (done < total * 0.7) { toast('Checklist incompleto (' + done + '/' + total + '). Completa al menos el 70%', 'warn'); return; }
  completarOrden(id);
}

function completarOrden(id) {
  var idx = STATE.ordenes.findIndex(function(o) { return o.id === id; });
  var updates = {estado:'completada', hora_fin:new Date().toISOString(), checklist_json:JSON.stringify(STATE.checklistState), valoracion:STATE.valoracion};
  if (idx >= 0) STATE.ordenes[idx] = Object.assign({}, STATE.ordenes[idx], updates);
  STATE.currentOrden = idx >= 0 ? STATE.ordenes[idx] : STATE.currentOrden;
  var badge = document.getElementById('dBadge');
  badge.textContent = 'Completada'; badge.className = 'detail-hbadge e-completada';
  renderDetalleBody(STATE.currentOrden);
  renderDash(); renderOrdenes();
  toast('¡Instalación completada! 🎉', 'ok');
}

function reportarIncidencia(id) {
  var motivo = prompt('Describe la incidencia:');
  if (!motivo) return;
  var idx = STATE.ordenes.findIndex(function(o) { return o.id === id; });
  if (idx >= 0) STATE.ordenes[idx] = Object.assign({}, STATE.ordenes[idx], {estado:'incidencia', notas_instalador:motivo});
  STATE.currentOrden = idx >= 0 ? STATE.ordenes[idx] : STATE.currentOrden;
  var badge = document.getElementById('dBadge');
  badge.textContent = 'Incidencia'; badge.className = 'detail-hbadge e-incidencia';
  renderDetalleBody(STATE.currentOrden);
  renderDash();
  toast('Incidencia reportada', 'warn');
}

function initCanvas() {
  var c = document.getElementById('canvasFirma');
  if (!c) return;
  var ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#0a1628'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.5; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  var drawing = false; var last = null;
  function getPos(e) {
    var r = c.getBoundingClientRect();
    var t = e.touches ? e.touches[0] : e;
    return { x: (t.clientX - r.left) * (c.width / r.width), y: (t.clientY - r.top) * (c.height / r.height) };
  }
  c.addEventListener('mousedown',  function(e) { drawing = true; last = getPos(e); });
  c.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) { e.preventDefault(); drawing = true; last = getPos(e); }, {passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('mousemove',  function(e) { if (!drawing) return; var p = getPos(e); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(last.x, last.y); ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.stroke(); last = p; });
  c.addEventListener('touchmove',  function(e) { e.preventDefault(); if (!drawing) return; var p = getPos(e); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(last.x, last.y); ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.stroke(); last = p; }, {passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('mouseup',  function() { drawing = false; });
  c.addEventListener('touchend', function() { drawing = false; });
}

function renderPerfil() {
  var mis = STATE.ordenes.filter(function(o) { return o.instalador_id === STATE.instaladorId; });
  var completadas = mis.filter(function(o) { return o.estado === 'completada'; });
  var vals = completadas.filter(function(o) { return o.valoracion > 0; }).map(function(o) { return +o.valoracion; });
  var valMedia = vals.length ? Math.round(vals.reduce(function(a, b) { return a + b; }, 0) / vals.length * 10) / 10 : 0;
  var zona = (INSTALADORES[STATE.instaladorId] || {}).zona || '—';
  s('psTotal', completadas.length);
  s('psValor', valMedia ? valMedia + '⭐' : '—');
  s('psZona', zona.split('/')[0].trim());
}

function initNotifs() {
  var hoy = new Date();
  var mis = STATE.ordenes.filter(function(o) { return o.instalador_id === STATE.instaladorId; });
  var hoyOrd   = mis.filter(function(o) { return o.fecha_programada && new Date(o.fecha_programada).toDateString() === hoy.toDateString(); });
  var urgentes = mis.filter(function(o) { return o.prioridad === 'urgente' && o.estado !== 'completada'; });
  var notifs = [];
  hoyOrd.forEach(function(o)   { notifs.push({ico:'fa-calendar-check', col:'var(--blue)', titulo:'Orden ' + o.numero_ot + ' programada para hoy', sub:escIns(o.cliente_nombre) + ' · ' + escIns(o.poblacion)}); });
  urgentes.forEach(function(o) { notifs.push({ico:'fa-exclamation-triangle', col:'var(--red)', titulo:'⚠️ URGENTE: ' + o.numero_ot, sub:escIns(o.cliente_nombre) + ' · Prioridad máxima'}); });
  if (!notifs.length) notifs.push({ico:'fa-check-circle', col:'var(--green)', titulo:'Sin notificaciones pendientes', sub:'Todo en orden'});
  STATE.notifs = notifs;
  var badge = document.getElementById('navBadgeNot');
  var n = notifs.filter(function(x) { return x.col !== 'var(--green)'; }).length;
  if (n > 0) { badge.style.display = 'flex'; badge.textContent = n; document.getElementById('notifDot').style.display = 'block'; }
  document.getElementById('notifsList').innerHTML = notifs.map(function(n) {
    return '<div style="background:#fff;border-radius:12px;border:1px solid var(--border);padding:13px 14px;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start"><div style="width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:50%;background:' + n.col + '18;color:' + n.col + ';display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:.9rem;flex-shrink:0"><i class="fas ' + n.ico + '"></i></div><div><div style="font-size:.82rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)">' + n.titulo + '</div><div style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--text2);margin-top:2px">' + n.sub + '</div></div></div>';
  }).join('');
}

function irScreen(id, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(function(s) { s.classList.remove('active'); });
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(function(b) { b.classList.remove('active'); });
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
}

function crearNuevaOrden() {
  var nombre  = document.getElementById('noNombre').value.trim();
  var tel     = document.getElementById('noTelefono').value.trim();
  var dir     = document.getElementById('noDireccion').value.trim();
  var pob     = document.getElementById('noPoblacion').value.trim();
  var kit     = document.getElementById('noKit').value;
  var fecha   = document.getElementById('noFecha').value;
  if (!nombre || !tel || !dir || !pob || !kit || !fecha) { toast('Rellena todos los campos obligatorios (*)', 'warn'); return; }
  var num  = 'OT-' + new Date().getFullYear() + '-' + String(Math.floor(Math.random() * 9000) + 1000);
  var body = {
    id: 'local_' + Date.now(),
    numero_ot: num,
    cliente_nombre: nombre, cliente_telefono: tel,
    contrato_num: document.getElementById('noContrato').value.trim() || '—',
    direccion: dir, poblacion: pob,
    cp: document.getElementById('noCp').value.trim() || '',
    kit_nombre: kit,
    instalador_id: STATE.instaladorId,
    instalador_nombre: STATE.instaladorNombre,
    estado: 'programada',
    prioridad: document.getElementById('noPrioridad').value,
    fecha_programada: new Date(fecha).toISOString(),
    notas_instalador: document.getElementById('noNotas').value.trim() || '',
    checklist_json: '{}', materiales: '', valoracion: 0
  };
  STATE.ordenes.unshift(body);
  ['noNombre','noTelefono','noContrato','noDireccion','noPoblacion','noCp','noNotas'].forEach(function(id) { var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = ''; });
  document.getElementById('noKit').selectedIndex = 0;
  document.getElementById('noPrioridad').selectedIndex = 0;
  document.getElementById('noFecha').value = '';
  renderDash(); renderOrdenes(); buildWeekDays();
  toast('Orden ' + num + ' creada ✅', 'ok');
  setTimeout(function() { irScreen('sOrdenes', document.getElementById('nb_ord')); }, 800);
}

function abrirMapa(dir, pob) { window.open('https://maps.google.com/?q=' + encodeURIComponent((dir||'') + ', ' + (pob||'') + ', España'), '_blank'); }
function llamarCliente(tel) { if (tel) window.open('tel:' + tel); }

function s(id, v) { var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.innerHTML = v; }
function fd(d) {
  if (!d) return '—';
  try { return new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {day:'2-digit', month:'2-digit', year:'numeric', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'}); }
  catch(e) { return '—'; }
}
function toast(msg, type) {
  var t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.className = 'toast' + (type ? ' ' + type : '');
  void t.offsetWidth;
  t.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(function() { t.classList.remove('show'); }, 3200);
}

if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
  window.addEventListener('load', function() {
    navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js')
      .then(function(r) { console.log('[SW] Instalador registrado, scope:', r.scope); })
      .catch(function(e) { console.warn('[SW] Error registro:', e); });
  });
}
